## Constructed root guarantee

`SourceCurrentConstructedRootCall.root_call` starts from a literal closed
FreshOrdinary build from the empty source heap. An actual original-field
allocation history must cover all expression guards. Its starting equality
interner must be valid. The modeled caller and its global memo tables
start logically empty. The inherited value is the actual empty allocation.

If the bounded root call returns, and Fits, carrier and scheduled Room
hold, its returned value is available and, for every ambient interpretation,

`L(result) = evaluate(original expression)`.

The statement covers the full source syntax, including nested raw and
smart binders and retained earlier cache writers. It supplies no semantic
Context, guard heap, Frame, root availability or desired result law.
`AllocatedPartialInitialization.constructed` derives the logical Context.
`ConstrainedCapGuardExecution.closed_heap` derives the initial guard heap.
`ConstructedOuterInitial.node_call` derives the actual NodeLaw.
`InternedGuardedExecution.closed_language` identifies the input language
with its independent least finite-tree syntax semantics.

This endpoint takes actual construction and root-call completion as
premises. The separate `InternedFreshOrdinaryClosedCompletion.closed_complete`
theorem supplies a completed fresh build from empty captures and caches for
each source expression in the model. General outer reduction completion
remains open. These results do not prove concrete Haskell cache startup,
host integer adequacy or whole-library termination.

## Broad reduction guarantee

For arbitrary inherited constraints, completed supported bounded reduction
has the exact law

`L(input) intersect Sat(inherited) <= L(result) <= L(input)`.

`SourceCurrentAllocatedPartialMemoSound.NodeLaw` states this contract.
`SourceCurrentOuterInitialActual.node_call` derives it from actual modeled
success under its Context, Frame, readiness and resource assumptions.
The constructed entry modules derive the startup assumptions for their
specified initial states. Arbitrary warm cache validity remains explicit.

This law retains every valid input tree and adds no input-external tree.
It does not assert that every returned tree satisfies inherited constraints.
Contradictory inheritance does not by itself imply an Empty result.

## Exact size queries

Actual closed modeled construction with valid annotations derives the
internal assumptions for the reference and optimized size-query paths.
`SourceFreshOrdinarySizedEntry.constructed_complete` proves both paths
finish with the same result and final cache. It retains errors.

If that result contains a query API, `SourceSizedApiLaws.Exact` gives exact
finite-tree classes by node count, no duplicate trees, exact counts,
inverse rank and unrank, and exact enumeration prefixes in nondecreasing
node-count order. Valid annotations and completed original construction
remain premises. Not every input is proved to return an API.

## Whole-library target and missing work

For each public operation, the target remains:

`Valid(input) and Supported(input, initial resources)`
`implies Haskell execution terminates and satisfies its specification`.

Support must be stated from initial data. It cannot assume future success.
The target includes construction, transformations, reduction, size classes,
counts, ranks, enumeration, generation, probability and external interfaces.
Their separate checked contracts do not establish whole-library refinement.

The largest mathematical gap is general outer completion. It needs
active-owner exclusion and bounds on fresh recursive calls after actual
native edits. A simple ordinary-height rank is already refuted by a
completed source-built example. A valid measure must respect physical memo
keys and inherited constraints. Repeated-pass convergence is separate.
Read `notes-current-outer-progress.md` for the exact obstruction and target.
